    15	
    16	## 问题（一句话）
    17	在双阶段 TDD（单上行探测块获取信道、随后连续发射 $Q=16$ 块相干下行波形）的高机动多用户 `MIMO-AFDM ISAC` 系统中，单基地雷达为分辨贴近慢速杂波带（$|\nu_c|\le 0.03$）的弱目标（如 $\nu_{\text{tar}}=0.145$）物理上必须维持连续 $Q=16$ 块慢时间相干处理间隔（CPI），但单块上行多普勒估计的 `CRLB` 残差与簇内微多普勒扩散（$\sigma_{\nu,\text{sub}}=0.025$）跨 16 块累积 $>180^\circ$ 各径随机相位翻转，令相干外推预编码（`Rang Liu & Schober 2026` 固定逐格 `WMMSE-MM` 与逐块外推 `RZF`）多用户干扰相消翻相长而崩溃，砍短为 $8\times(Q=2)$ 子 CPI 重测虽能缓解老化却因 2 块杂波投影直接损失 `5.5–6.2 dB` 目标能量与相干增益而使盲搜检测率归零；能否在零额外下行控制信令与不打断 16 块雷达相干 CPI 的前提下，靠路径空间解耦与单格共址波束匹配叠加导频同时打穿跨块老化平层与同预算盲信道跟踪瓶颈？
    18	
    19	## 白话与类比
    20	基站用一次上行导频测出 3 个高速移动用户的多径信道，紧接着要一口气连发 16 个下行数据块——因为基站雷达必须攒够连续 16 块的慢时间回波，才能在去杂波投影时保住贴近杂波边缘的慢速弱目标（开发集实测 16 块投影目标能量仅损 `0.74 dB`，而切成 8 段各 2 块子 CPI 做静态杂波陷波会直接切掉 `5.45–6.24 dB` 目标能量，再叠加非相干损失使全图盲搜检出率跌至 `0%`）。
    21	
    22	可连发 16 块时，每条路径微小的多普勒误差（`0.025`）滚过 19 个符号周期后相位翻转 $180^\circ$，现有办法在两头撞墙：
    23	1. **跨块相干预编码与逐径对齐**：无论是 `Rang Liu & Schober 2026` 的 CPI 固定逐格 `WMMSE-MM`、逐块慢时间相位外推 `ExtrapBlockRZF`（固定块内散射只把 CSI 年龄从 `q=0` 拉到 16 块，`SER` 即从 `0.251` 崩至 `0.637`），还是把本用户多径预延时对齐到同一抽头的 `DDAM`（`Lu & Zeng TWC'24`，三径老化相位在同格相干自抵消，`SER = 0.366`），都扛不住跨块相位失相干；
    24	2. **跨波形同结构盲跟踪瓶颈（经 `gpt-6.2-sol` 独立 `True-H` 诊断收窄归因）**：若基站改用对各干扰路径角做空间白化置零的路径解耦波束 $\mathbf{w}_k$，并在同一导频位置叠加对全体用户置零的雷达波束 $\mathbf{w}_{\text{tar}}$ 与各用户同波束导频 $\sum_k \sqrt{E_{u,\text{pil}}}\mathbf{w}_k$，当白送真实有效信道（`True-H`）并配全块 `LMMSE` 时，`AFDM`（`0.00%`）与梳状 `OFDM`（`Sol` 第 6 步复核读数 `0.075%`、本工作树开发集 20 场景校准读数 `0.13%`）均几乎无错——**跨波形差距不在已知信道均衡层，而严格锁定在同开销（`24/64` 导频保护格、`39/64` 净 16-QAM 载荷 + `1/64` 参考符号）下的盲逐块有效多径信道估计层**：`AFDM` 靠单一脉冲导频在仿射域按 $m_p - 2Nc_1\ell_p$ 将各径搬入互不重叠的局部窗，避开数据区泄漏与跨径混叠（`SER = 4.11%`）；而等开销梳状 `OFDM`（即便装上 `gpt-6.2-sol` 补强的保护格降权 `0.6` 三轮正交投影联合非线性最小二乘估计器）在 $|\nu|\le 0.85$ 数据 `ICI` 灌入导频及多径频域混叠下仍卡在 `19.78%`（爬山子集 `38.53%`）。
    25	
    26	## 锚点（出处）
    27	1. `Rang Liu, Ming Li, A. L. Swindlehurst, N. Franchi & R. Schober, "Multiuser MIMO-AFDM Beamforming for ISAC in Doubly Dispersive Channels," arXiv:2607.05119, Jul. 2026`（Sec. IV-B Eqs. 32–46 逐格 CPI 固定 `WMMSE-MM` 与 Sec. V：$N_t=6\text{–}12, N_r=6, N=64, Q=16, K=3$ 位于 $\{-45^\circ, -10^\circ, 20^\circ\}$，$P_k=3$，$\theta_t=40^\circ$；已在 L2 脚本启动自检忠实复现其 $\nu_{\max}=0.1, \text{SNR}=15\text{ dB}$ 下单调收敛轨迹 `6.22 → 11.19 bps/Hz`）。
    28	2. `Shuntian Tang, Xinyi Wang, Shuangyang Li, Tianqi Mao, Zilong Liu & Zesong Fei, "Probabilistic Symbol-Level Precoding based Affine Frequency Division Multiplexing Transmission," arXiv:2609.09644, Sep. 2026`（Table I：$N=64, P=3, l_{\max}=2, \alpha_{\max}=1$，双阶段 TDD 上行估计接下行预编码）及同组 `IEEE TWC 2026` (`10.1109/twc.2026.3692638`)。
    29	3. `Hongao Lu & Yong Zeng, "Delay-Doppler Alignment Modulation for Spatially Sparse Massive MIMO Communication," IEEE TWC, 2024` (`10.1109/twc.2024.3349425`)、`Z. Xiao, Y. Zeng et al., IEEE ICC 2023` (`10.1109/icc45041.2023.10279599`) 及 `S. Rivetti et al., arXiv:2601.05032v5, 2026`（双时间尺度对齐与统计导频时隙分离的跨领域先例）。
    30	
    31	## 去壳三句
    32	1. **谁真卡住了（点名现在的做法或论文）**：在 `Liu & Schober 2026` ($Q=16, K=3, P_k=3, N=64$) 结合 `Tang et al. 2026` ($\nu_{\max}=0.85, l_{\max}=2$，双阶段 TDD) 的工况下，固定完全相同的块内多普勒与角展宽，仅将 CSI 年龄从无老化单步拉长至 16 块 CPI，外推逐块迫零 `ExtrapBlockRZF_AFDM` 的 `SER` 即从 `0.2513` 恶化至 `0.6369`（`Liu 2026` 原版 CPI 固定逐格 `WMMSE-MM` 更因单波束跨 16 块失配崩至 `>0.70`）；若改为每 2 块重测上行的 `8×(Q=2)` 短子 CPI（配满 16 块总雷达能量、无任何消峰删行），每次 2 块静态杂波正交投影直接损耗 `5.45 dB` 目标模板能量（16 块仅损 `0.74 dB`），全图等虚警带峰位核对 `LocBlindPd` 从 `100.0%` 跌至 **`0.0%`**。
    33	2. **解决了会改变什么设计决定；换成最朴素但不犯傻的做法，损失多大**：使高机动 TDD `MIMO-AFDM ISAC` 无需砍短雷达相干 CPI 或每块插上行反馈，即可在 16 块连续 CPI 上同时保住慢速弱目标盲搜检测与高速 16-QAM 通信。三种替代均显著退化：(a) 修正保护区零外溢并配齐同预算全块 `LMMSE` 的 `DDAM`（`Lu & Zeng TWC'24`）因三径对齐至同格后跨块随机相位相消，`SER = 0.3664`；(b) 零侧信息泄漏双向消融 `A-only`（有路径解耦波束、无逐块导频重估）`SER = 0.8276`（`0%` 增益），`B-only`（有共址导频逐块盲重估、基站改用无跨用户置零的 `MRT` 多径波束）`SER = 0.2425`（仅收回 `66.2% < 80%` 增益，`SER` 恶化 `5.9×`）；(c) 等净载荷（`39/64`）梳状 `OFDM_Comb_JointWLS`（采用 `gpt-6.2-sol` 提供的保护格 `0.6` 降权 + 三轮 QR 正交投影联合非线性最小二乘估计 + 全块精确 `LMMSE`，`Z=0` 静态 `SER = 0.82% < 1%`）在高机动下因数据 `ICI` 污染导频与多径同频混叠致盲信道估计退化，`SER = 0.1978`（较本卡 `0.0411` 高 **`4.81×`**，几何均值比 **`5.40×`**，且本卡在低 `5 dB` 信噪比即 `23 dB` 下仍优于 28 dB 的全部 `OFDM` 与外推对手，净赢 `> 5.0 dB`）。
    34	3. **难处的结构是什么（为什么现有办法过不去；靠什么让难处消失）**：难处在**雷达慢时间去杂波要求的 16 块长相干窗**与**多径复增益相位的块级失相干**锁死，且在去除了跨用户路径干扰后，**单天线终端能否在每块固定导频预算内从双色散观测中无混叠重估自身多径信道**决定了最终地板（旧版卡面曾将对 `OFDM` 的优势归因于三径频选衰落，经 `gpt-6.2-sol` 第 6 步独立 `True-H` 实验证伪［`Sol` 复核 `True-H OFDM SER = 0.075%`，开发集校准 `0.13%`］并收窄更正为**同开销共址导频下的仿射分径盲信道估计优势**）：
    35	   - 基站利用 $N_t > KP_k$（如 $N_t=12 > 9$）构造跨用户路径扇区白化波束 $\mathbf{w}_k$ 与对全用户路径置零的雷达波束 $\mathbf{w}_{\text{tar}}$（开发集实测外来导频串扰 `-28.6 dB`），在单一 DAFT 格 $m_p=10$ 上跨 16 块恒定叠加 $\mathbf{s}_{\text{pil}}[m_p] = \alpha_{\text{rad}}e^{j\psi}\mathbf{w}_{\text{tar}} + \sum_k \sqrt{E_{u,\text{pil}}}\mathbf{w}_k$（精确二次根配平总导频能量 $E_{\text{pil}}$）；
    36	   - 在 `AFDM` ($2Nc_1=4$) 中，脉冲导频 $m_p=10$ 经 $\ell\in\{0,1,2\}$ 确定性分裂至互不重叠的局部窗 $\{10, 6, 2\}$ 且数据区 `D_af=[22..61]` 左移后严格落在 `[14..61]` 不侵入 `obs_af=[0..12]`，使单天线用户无需任何下行控制信令即可逐块高精度恢复 $(g_{k,p}(q), \nu_{k,p}(q))$；而在 `OFDM (c1=c2=0)` 中，40 个数据子载波在 $|\nu|\le 0.85$ 下的块内 `ICI` 直接灌入梳状导频且三径在同组导频子载波上混叠，锁死盲信道估计地板。
    37	
    38	## 最接近的已有工作
    39	1. `Rang Liu, Ming Li, A. L. Swindlehurst, N. Franchi & R. Schober, "Multiuser MIMO-AFDM Beamforming for ISAC in Doubly Dispersive Channels," arXiv:2607.05119, Jul. 2026`（**已读全文并忠实复现其 WMMSE-MM**）：假设全 16 块瞬时下行 CSI 完美已知且 CPI 内固定波束，在跨块老化下崩溃。
    40	2. `Shuntian Tang, Xinyi Wang, Shuangyang Li, Tianqi Mao, Zilong Liu & Zesong Fei, "Probabilistic Symbol-Level Precoding based AFDM Transmission," arXiv:2609.09644, Sep. 2026`（**已读全文**）与 `Tang & Fei et al., IEEE TWC 2026` (`10.1109/twc.2026.3692638`)：单天线单块 SISO TDD 预编码，未解多块雷达 CPI 老化与多用户盲跟踪。
    41	3. `Hongao Lu & Yong Zeng, "Delay-Doppler Alignment Modulation for Spatially Sparse Massive MIMO Communication," IEEE TWC, 2024` (`10.1109/twc.2024.3349425`) 与 `Z. Xiao, Y. Zeng et al., IEEE ICC 2023` (`10.1109/icc45041.2023.10279599`)：逐径预延时对齐至同抽头，在跨块相位老化下因同格多径随机相位相消退化（修正零外溢并配全块均衡后 `SER = 36.6%`）。
    42	4. `S. Rivetti et al., arXiv:2601.05032v5, 2026`（由 `gpt-6.2-sol` 检出补入）：`OFDM` 通感下考虑信道老化与运动杂波并将感知波束投至用户零空间，但其导频时隙用统计特征波束而数据时隙用另一套预编码（分时且不同波束），未构造同一仿射格叠加零空间雷达波束与复用数据波束的用户逐块多径跟踪接口。
    43	5. `F. Zhang, Z. Wang, T. Mao et al., IEEE JSAC, vol. 44, pp. 310–324, 2026` (`10.1109/jsac.2025.3609766`) 与 `H. Yin et al., IEEE TWC, 2024` (`10.1109/twc.2024.3361679`)：点对点保护带导频或上行 $K$ 窗正交导频，未利用下行发射空间解耦实现 $K$ 用户与雷达单格共址零控制开销复用。
    44	
    45	## 价值与代价
    46	- **价值（`value: 3`）**：打通高机动多用户 TDD `MIMO-AFDM ISAC` 在 16 块连续雷达 CPI 下的跨块老化死锁，在严格等净载荷（`39/64` 16-QAM）、零侧信息泄漏与纯线性 `ExactSubspaceBLUE` 雷达评分下，相对 `gpt-6.2-sol` 强化后的联合拟合梳状 `OFDM` 降低误码率 `4.8×–5.4×`（且在低 `5 dB` 发射信噪比下仍胜出，远超破壁账 `1.0 dB` 门槛），同时消除 `8×(Q=2)` 短子 CPI 的 `5.45 dB` 杂波投影目标损失（`LocBlindPd: 0% → 100%`）。
    47	- **适用范围与代价（诚实边界）**：
    48	  1. **阵元自由度前提**：需 $N_t > KP_k$（如 $N_t=12, K=3, P_k=3$ 或 $N_t=16, K=4, P_k=3$）以留出用户路径置零后的雷达专用空间自由度；富散射（$KP_k \ge N_t$）或不同用户主路径共角（$<2^\circ$）时不适用。
    49	  2. **结论层级锁定（Iron Rule 1）**：已知有效信道（`True-H`）下全块均衡 `OFDM` 与 `AFDM` 均低于 `0.2%` `SER`，故本卡对等开销 `OFDM` 的净赢严格归因于**双色散块内 `ICI` 下单脉冲仿射分径导频相对频域梳状导频的盲有效信道跟踪增益**，不宣称已知信道检测分集增益。
    50	- **最便宜致命检验（L2 预注册靶点，`ideas/l2/PREDICTIONS_h_r11_h02.md`）**：在 `deinlab`（8 核 `AFDM` 环境，全新独立测试种子 `200000+ / 300000+ / 400000+`，与开发集 `900..9999` 物理隔离）跑 `n_cal=320, n_val=320, n_pos_Z0=64, n_pos_Z1=144`，以配对 95% 置信区间下限及实测半宽 `≤ 25%` 门槛距离判定 `PASS_PRIMARY`。
    51	
    52	## 筛选记录
    53	- 2026-11-25 L1 深想：`[高要求] [轨B-ISAC] 方法：跨层时间尺度错位解耦 + 共址空间正交叠加 账本：破壁账 + 跨层/通感倒挂账`。针对 `Rang Liu & Schober arXiv:2607.05119` 与 `Tang et al. TWC'26 / arXiv:2609.09644` 在 16 块连续 CPI 下的跨块老化死锁完成初版探针 `ideas/probes/h_probe_h02_aging_dual_isac.py`。
    54	- 2026-11-25 第 6 步异族审查与 REVISE 闭环（`gpt-6.2-sol` high，`ideas/reviews/r11-sol-step6-h02-20261125.md`）：`Sol` 查新判 **`(1a)` 未占**，独立复算算子误差 `7.38e-16`，同时给出关键 `True-H` 归因读数（`True-H OFDM SER = 0.075%`）与保护格 `0.6` 降权联合非线性最小二乘 `OFDM` 估计器（将 `OFDM_Comb` 静态压至 `0.55% < 1%`、高机动压至 `19.17%` vs `AFDM 3.42%`），并列出进 L2 前 3 项修正条件。已按规则 20 全数落实并经开发集校准核验（`dev_smoke_h02_revise.py`）：① 忠实复现 `Liu 2026` 原文逐格 `WMMSE-MM` 单调和速率轨迹 `[6.22 → 11.19] bps/Hz` 并单列 `Liu26_PerBinWMMSE_Aged` 与 `ExtrapBlockRZF_Aged`，改用 Frobenius 确定性归一 + 带内 `Dt[0]` 参考符号彻底根除 `beta_save` 泄漏（等净载荷 `39/64`），修正 `DDAM` 数据窗零外溢并配全块 `LMMSE`，将 `B-only` 改为零泄漏 `MRT` 波束；② 并入 `Sol` 的联合加权 `OFDM` 估计器，锁定盲信道估计归因，增设固定块内色散仅改 CSI 年龄的配对隔离臂；③ 去除全部 CLEAN 删峰与行掩码，改用纯线性 `ExactSubspaceBLUE` 并在满 16 块能量下对比 `8×(Q=2)` 子 CPI。
    55	- 2026-11-25 L2 跑前异族静态审计放行（`gpt-6.2-sol`，`ideas/reviews/r11-sol-prel2-h02-20261125.md` 至 `v6`）：逐项修净终端侧信息读取（除预注册明文赠予 `Liu26` 基站合并矩阵外全盲本地观测）、`DDAM` `[19..58]` vs `[4..8]` 保护窗与信漏噪比正则、逐块期望总能量精确等于 `64.0`、雷达 `Q=16` 与 `Q=2` 统一五频点 `1e-6` QR 投影与总能量比 `proj_keep_db`、以及 Newcombe Method 10 配对二元区间与 25% 半宽硬门限，获 `PRE-L2 AUDIT VERDICT: CLEARED FOR DEINLAB L2` 后提交冻结提交 `a533a7d85`。
    56	- 2026-11-25 第 7 步 `deinlab` 8 核正式 L2 实测（`AFDM` 环境，`147.35 s`，汇总文件 `ideas/l2/out_h_r11_h02/summary_h_r11_h02.json`）：
    57	  1. **观测读数（`N_pos_Z0=64, N_pos_Z1=144, N_cal=320, N_val=320`）**：

---
name: research-checkpoint
description: Use at four moments in any research project — (1) before freezing an experiment protocol or launching a pre-registered run, (2) before reporting results or writing/accepting a result card, (3) when a result contradicts expectation or you are about to explain a result, (4) when choosing or changing research direction or the paper's main line. Gives a short checklist for each moment.
---

# Research checkpoint

These are short checklists. Do the one for the current moment, write its answers into the artifact (protocol, card,
report or decision brief), then continue. Background: `../../docs/methods-handbook.md`
and `../../RULES_MAP.md` (relative to this skill's source directory; resolve its symlink first).

## 1. Before freezing a protocol or launching a pre-registered run

1. **Competing hypotheses.** Write down 2–3 alternatives and what each predicts for every arm. If all of them predict the
   same thing, the experiment cannot discriminate: redesign or drop it.
2. **Design validity, four questions.**
   - (a) Does each contrast change exactly one factor? If the conclusion will credit a component (a term, a module, a prior),
     is there an arm that removes it entirely, and is the simplest baseline in? If not, write why. The external design review
     (item 5) checks this too. Handbook A12.
   - (b) Leverage: from existing data or gate units only, is the expected difference clearly above noise? If a hypothesis
     predicts no difference, write an equivalence prediction.
   - (c) Statistical unit: cluster at the level where randomness is shared.
   - (d) What exactly does every proxy or oracle mean?
3. **Premortem.** If the result came out opposite to expectation, what is the most likely reason, and does the design
   already rule it out?
4. **Prediction ledger.** Record your own predictions with probabilities.
5. If the conclusion will be stated as a **mechanism**, get one short external review of the design and decision rule,
   using a different model, before freezing.
6. Verify every gate statement against the code. Commit before running. Run long jobs as one unattended chain with a cost
   stop and a DONE/STOPPED status line, and do not poll.
7. Optional, judge per case: would a small slice answer the decision question earlier? If the run is costly or its
   answer may change the plan, offer a staged look (pilot slice where events are dense, stop at the first useful result,
   report). Not for confirmatory claims. See `docs/methods-handbook.md` B8.

## 2. Before reporting results or writing a result card

- Report in three layers:
  1. observation (numbers);
  2. pre-registered verdict, stated as frozen and never relabelled;
  3. candidate explanation, marked *untested* with an H/M/L confidence.
- Credit an effect to a component only against a neutral control (its removal or the simplest working baseline), and only
  if the comparator is not worse than no intervention; otherwise say "A beats B", not "X causes it". When removal is
  ill-defined, say why and use the simplest working baseline.
- Say "unified", "proven" or "refuted" only after a pre-registered or prospective test has passed.
- Correct earlier findings with explicit correction notes; never rewrite them silently.
- Score the prediction-ledger entries for this run.
- Before writing shared records (IDs, state files, indexes), check what is already there.

## 3. When a result contradicts expectation

1. **Audit before explaining**: check data consistency across experiments, confounds and the statistical unit. If the
   surprise is a difference between two pipelines, first list every stage that differs and toggle the unintended ones
   (handbook B9).
2. Offer at most **one** new explanation, together with its cheapest falsifying test. Do not stack explanations on
   untested ones.
3. Optionally, ask a different model for up to two explanations without showing it yours, then compare.

## 4. When choosing or changing direction or the main line

1. Write your own assessment and commit it first.
2. Ask a different model for an independent assessment. Give it an input bundle: the goal, the primary evidence (result cards
   including drafts, open anomalies, the negative-results ledger) and nothing of your assessment or framing. It states the
   problem in its own words first, and gives at least one rival explanation that needs neither the main component nor the
   main claim. Trigger an extra outside audit on events, not on a calendar: a premise of the main claim is falsified, the
   claim survives only by narrowing twice, an unexplained tie or reversal stays open, and once before writing starts.
   Choose the model by capability for the task.
3. When you relay other models' analyses, list each source's own conclusion and every point with its source, and state
   adopt / modify / reject with a reason before merging; afterwards check that every point appears or is marked rejected.
   Then make the divergence table. The user decides. Do not run multi-round debates.
4. Value decisions (starting a line, changing direction, calling a result Transactions-level or a principle), handbook A13:
   - Headroom first: in the target regime, measure the simplest sane baseline, our method and a genie or bound. Look for a
     principle only where the baseline is clearly short of the genie and fails for a nameable reason.
   - Who already does it: a table of the 5–10 closest papers, one plain sentence each with equation or section location;
     the user spot-checks two rows against the source. A "nobody does X" claim lists its search terms and the three closest
     papers.
   - Case for and case against, by two different models from the same bundle, each citing checkable evidence and stating
     what would change its mind. A third pass names only the 1–3 factual cruxes and the cheapest check for each. Decide by
     checking the cruxes, not by which case reads better. Offer 「白话」 for all three.
   - A "no" parks the idea in the open-threads list with a revival condition; a "yes" passes a cheap kill test before
     large investment.
   - Suggest showing the three plain sentences to the advisor or a senior student.
   Not for routine experiments, status reports or small decisions inside a settled direction.
5. Use the top-level checks: importance (Hamming), feasibility × interest (Alon), Heilmeier questions, and the outside
   view (base rates).
6. Every recommendation gives the strongest alternative, the evidence that would change it, and a confidence level.
---
name: trellis-start
description: "Initializes an AI development session by reading workflow guides, developer identity, git status, active tasks, and project guidelines from .trellis/. Classifies incoming tasks and routes to brainstorm, direct edit, or task workflow. Use when beginning a new coding session, resuming work, starting a new task, or re-establishing project context."
---

# Start Session

Initialize a Trellis-managed development session. This platform has no session-start hook, so manually load the equivalent compact context by following these steps.

---

## Step 1: Current state
Identity, git status, current task, active tasks, journal location.

```bash
python3 ./.trellis/scripts/get_context.py
```

If this output includes a line beginning `Trellis update available:`, copy the full line verbatim when summarizing session context. Do not shorten operational command hints.

## Step 2: Workflow overview
Compact Phase Index, request triage rules, planning artifact contract, and the step-detail command.

```bash
python3 ./.trellis/scripts/get_context.py --mode phase
```

Full guide in `.trellis/workflow.md` (read on demand).

## Step 3: Guideline indexes
Discover packages + spec layers, then read each relevant index file.

```bash
python3 ./.trellis/scripts/get_context.py --mode packages
cat .trellis/spec/guides/index.md
cat .trellis/spec/<package>/<layer>/index.md   # for each relevant layer
```

Index files list the specific guideline docs to read when you actually start coding.

## Step 4: Decide next action
From Step 1 you know the current task and status. Check the task directory:

- **Active task status `planning` + no `prd.md`** → Phase 1.1. Load the `trellis-brainstorm` skill.
- **Active task status `planning` + `prd.md` exists** → stay in Phase 1. Lightweight tasks can be PRD-only; complex tasks need `design.md` + `implement.md`. Load the relevant Phase 1 step detail before `task.py start`.
- **Active task status `in_progress`** → Phase 2 step 2.1. Load the step detail:
  ```bash
  python3 ./.trellis/scripts/get_context.py --mode phase --step 2.1 --platform codex
  ```
- **No active task** → classify first. For simple conversation / small task, ask only whether this turn should create a Trellis task. For complex work, ask whether you may create a Trellis task and enter planning. If the user says no, skip Trellis for this session.

---

## Skill routing (quick reference)

| User intent | Skill |
|---|---|
| New feature / unclear requirements | `trellis-brainstorm` |
| About to write code | `trellis-before-dev` |
| Done coding / quality check | `trellis-check` |
| Stuck / fixed same bug multiple times | `trellis-break-loop` |
| Learned something worth capturing | `trellis-update-spec` |

Full rules + anti-rationalization table in `.trellis/workflow.md`.

  1005	def newcombe_paired_binary_ci95(x_hits, y_hits):
  1006	    """
  1007	    Newcombe (1998, Stat. Med. 17:2635-2650, Method 10) Wilson score 95% CI for paired binary difference p1 - p2.
  1008	    Remains well-defined with non-zero finite Wilson width even when p1=1.0 and p2=0.0!
  1009	    """
  1010	    x = np.asarray(x_hits, dtype=float) > 0.5

  1013	    z = 1.959964

  1015	        return {"mean": 0.0, "ci95_low": 0.0, "ci95_high": 0.0, "ci95_half_width": 0.0}
  1016

  1018	    b = float(np.sum(x & (~y)))
  1019	    c = float(np.sum((~x) & y))

  1026	        den = 1.0 + (z * z) / n
  1027	        cen = (p + (z * z) / (2.0 * n)) / den
  1028	        rad = (z * np.sqrt(max(p * (1.0 - p) / n + (z * z) / (4.0 * n * n), 0.0))) / den
  1029	        return max(0.0, cen - rad), min(1.0, cen + rad)

  1036	        corr = (num - np.sign(num) * min(0.5 * n, abs(num))) / np.sqrt(m_prod)

  1096	def bootstrap_recovery_ci95(base_arr, abl_arr, prop_arr, n_boot=4000, seed_b=99999):
  1097	    rng_b = np.random.default_rng(seed_b)

  1102	    n = len(b)
  1103	    if n < 2:
  1104	        return {"mean": pt, "ci95_low": pt, "ci95_high": pt, "ci95_half_width": 0.0}
  1105	    idx = rng_b.integers(0, n, size=(n_boot, n))
  1106	    num = np.mean(b[idx] - ab[idx], axis=1)
  1107	    den = np.maximum(np.mean(b[idx] - pr[idx], axis=1), 1e-8)
  1108	    boots = num / den
  1109	    cl = float(np.quantile(boots, 0.025))
  1110	    ch = float(np.quantile(boots, 0.975))
  1111	    return {"mean": pt, "ci95_low": cl, "ci95_high": ch, "ci95_half_width": 0.5 * (ch - cl)}

    55	   - `Newcombe_CI95_lower(LocBlindPd_Prop_Q16 - LocBlindPd_Short8xQ2) >= 0.55` **AND** `ci95_half_width <= 0.1375` under unified QR clutter-projected RD scoring (`identical fn_clut, identical 1e-6 QR rank threshold, zero peak mask, total energy ratio proj_keep_db`), **AND**
    56	   - `CI95_upper(recovery_Aonly) < 0.80` (`hw <= 0.050`), `CI95_upper(recovery_Bonly_MRT) < 0.80` (`hw <= 0.050`), `CI95_lower(SER_Aonly - SER_Prop_AFDM) >= 0.080` (`hw <= 0.020`), and `CI95_lower(SER_Bonly_MRT - SER_Prop_AFDM) >= 0.080` (`hw <= 0.020`).
    57	
    58	---
    59	
    60	## 3. Diagnostic & Boundary Readouts (`SCOPE_READOUTS` — Unchanged, descriptive)
    61	
    62	1. **`True-H` Attribution Readout**: `Oracle_TrueEffH_AFDM` vs `Oracle_TrueEffH_OFDM_Comb` `SER` at `Z=0` and `Z=1`.
    63	2. **Pure CSI-Aging Isolation Readout**: Paired `ExtrapBlockRZF_UnagedQ0_AFDM` vs `ExtrapBlockRZF_Aged_AFDM` vs `Prop_BeamMatchedDualISAC_AFDM`.
    64	3. **Radar Total Target Energy Retention (`proj_keep_db`)**: `10 * log10(sum(tot_proj_e) / sum(tot_raw_e))` for `Q=16` vs `8×(Q=2)`.
    65	4. **Second Antenna/User Scaling Regime (`Nt = 16, K = 4, Pk = 3`, `SNR = 26 dB`, `N_pos = 48`)**.
    66	
    67	---
    68	
    69	## 4. Research-Checkpoint Pre-Freeze Protocol Audit (`research-checkpoint` §1 & `round-12.md` Blocking ⑤)
    70	
    71	1. **Competing Hypotheses**:
    72	   - **`H_main` (Physical 16-block phase aging + blind affine pilot tracking)**: 4-round truncation in Round 11 only caused `Liu26` under-convergence in `Z=0` static scenes with close user path angles; at 20 WMMSE rounds `Liu26` converges to `< 1.0%` at `Z=0`, remains broken (`> 65%` SER) at `Z=1` due to `> 180°` slow-time phase aging, and `Prop` passes all `P1` and `P2` gates (`PASS_PRIMARY = true`).
    73	   - **`H_alt1` (WMMSE iteration deficiency explained dynamic `Z=1` gap)**: Once `Liu26` runs 20 WMMSE rounds, its dynamic `Z=1` SER drops near `Prop`, violating `CI95_lower(SER_Liu26 - SER_Prop) >= 0.150` (`PASS_PRIMARY = false`).
    74	   - **`H_alt2` (Seed-specific false-alarm / ablation fluctuation)**: On unseen `500000..740047` seeds, either `val_Pfa_blind` drifts outside `[0.015, 0.095]` or `Abl_Bonly_MRT` recovery 95% upper bound reaches `>= 0.80`, yielding `PASS_PRIMARY = false` and immediate `killed`.
    75	2. **Design Validity & Paired Single-Mechanism Controls (Blocking ⑤)**:
    76	   - (a) *Single-factor contrasts*: All arms share identical channel realizations, Stage-1 sounding (`est_s1`), `39/64` payload symbols, `24/64` pilot+guard overhead, `P_tot = 64.0`, and thermal noise. `Prop` vs `OFDM_Comb_JointWLS_DualISAC` shares identical spatial beams `W_beams`, pilot energy, and full-block `LMMSE` backend, differing solely in `AFDM` single-impulse affine pilot vs `OFDM` comb pilot (`Oracle_TrueEffH_*` further isolates known-channel equalization). `ExtrapBlockRZF_UnagedQ0` vs `Aged` isolates CSI age alone; `Prop(Q=16)` vs `Short8xQ2` isolates CPI length under identical QR clutter projection; `Abl_Aonly` removes only per-block pilot tracking and `Abl_Bonly_MRT` removes only inter-user spatial path nulling.
    77	   - (b) *Leverage*: Every primary effect has `>= 2×` expected margin over its threshold and `<= 25%` CI half-width at `N_pos_Z0=64, N_pos_Z1=144`.
    78	   - (c) *Statistical unit*: Independent scene `seed_i` (clustering all `K` users and `Q=16` blocks per CPI).
    79	   - (d) *Oracle meaning*: `Oracle_TrueEffH_*` supplies exact instantaneous effective channel and exact pilot echo to `lmmse_slice_payload`, measuring the known-channel detection floor (`< 0.1%` for both waveforms).
    80	3. **Premortem**: With `Liu26` static convergence verified (`0.462%` on 40 dev scenes), the remaining non-trivial failure risks are finite-sample `val_Pfa_blind` (`N_val=320`, target `0.05`, gate `[0.015, 0.095]`) and `Abl_Bonly_MRT` recovery (`< 0.80`). Per `round-12.md`, neither gate nor sample size is loosened; any failure kills `h02` immediately.
    81	4. **Pre-Run Subjective Predictions**:
    82	   - `P(z0_all_below_1pct) = 0.96`, `P(pfa_hygiene_ok) = 0.97`, `P(P1) = 0.99`, `P(P2) = 0.95` → **`P(PASS_PRIMARY == true) = 0.88`**.

  1005	def newcombe_paired_binary_ci95(x_hits, y_hits):
  1006	    """
  1007	    Newcombe (1998, Stat. Med. 17:2635-2650, Method 10) Wilson score 95% CI for paired binary difference p1 - p2.
  1008	    Remains well-defined with non-zero finite Wilson width even when p1=1.0 and p2=0.0!
  1009	    """
  1010	    x = np.asarray(x_hits, dtype=float) > 0.5
  1011	    y = np.asarray(y_hits, dtype=float) > 0.5
  1012	    n = float(len(x))
  1013	    z = 1.959964
  1014	    if n < 1:
  1015	        return {"mean": 0.0, "ci95_low": 0.0, "ci95_high": 0.0, "ci95_half_width": 0.0}
  1016	
  1017	    a = float(np.sum(x & y))
  1018	    b = float(np.sum(x & (~y)))
  1019	    c = float(np.sum((~x) & y))
  1020	    d = float(np.sum((~x) & (~y)))
  1021	    p1 = (a + b) / n
  1022	    p2 = (a + c) / n
  1023	    diff = p1 - p2
  1024	
  1025	    def _wilson(p):
  1026	        den = 1.0 + (z * z) / n
  1027	        cen = (p + (z * z) / (2.0 * n)) / den
  1028	        rad = (z * np.sqrt(max(p * (1.0 - p) / n + (z * z) / (4.0 * n * n), 0.0))) / den
  1029	        return max(0.0, cen - rad), min(1.0, cen + rad)
  1030	
  1031	    l1, u1 = _wilson(p1)
  1032	    l2, u2 = _wilson(p2)
  1033	    m_prod = (a + b) * (c + d) * (a + c) * (b + d)
  1034	    if m_prod > 0.0:
  1035	        num = a * d - b * c
  1036	        corr = (num - np.sign(num) * min(0.5 * n, abs(num))) / np.sqrt(m_prod)
  1037	    else:
  1038	        corr = 0.0
  1039	
  1040	    dl = np.sqrt(max((p1 - l1) ** 2 - 2.0 * corr * (p1 - l1) * (u2 - p2) + (u2 - p2) ** 2, 0.0))
  1041	    du = np.sqrt(max((u1 - p1) ** 2 - 2.0 * corr * (u1 - p1) * (p2 - l2) + (p2 - l2) ** 2, 0.0))
  1042	    ci_low = float(max(-1.0, diff - dl))
  1043	    ci_high = float(min(1.0, diff + du))
  1044	    # Use conservative max(dl, du) as half-width check so boundary asymmetry cannot understate width:
  1045	    hw_cons = float(max(diff - ci_low, ci_high - diff))
  1046	    return {"mean": float(diff), "ci95_low": ci_low, "ci95_high": ci_high, "ci95_half_width": hw_cons}
  1047

  1049	def run_regime_pool(pool, nu_max, sig_nu_sub, sig_th_sub, snr_db, Nt_v, K_v, n_cal, n_val, n_pos, seed_base, include_low26=False):
  1050	    cal_tasks = [(seed_base + i, "cal", False, nu_max, sig_nu_sub, sig_th_sub, snr_db, Nt_v, K_v, False) for i in range(n_cal)]
  1051	    val_tasks = [(seed_base + 20000 + i, "val", False, nu_max, sig_nu_sub, sig_th_sub, snr_db, Nt_v, K_v, False) for i in range(n_val)]
  1052	    pos_tasks = [(seed_base + 40000 + i, "pos", True, nu_max, sig_nu_sub, sig_th_sub, snr_db, Nt_v, K_v, include_low26) for i in range(n_pos)]

    49	def make_E_op(U_mat, c1_v, ell, nu):
    50	    g = np.ones(N, dtype=complex)
    51	    if ell > 0:
    52	        ix = np.arange(ell)
    53	        g[ix] = np.exp(-1j * 2 * np.pi * c1_v * (N ** 2 - 2 * N * (ell - ix)))
    54	    phase_d = np.exp(1j * 2 * np.pi * nu * np.arange(N) / float(N)) * g
    55	    return (U_mat * phase_d[None, :]) @ np.roll(U_mat.conj().T, ell, axis=0)

    58	def solve_exact_expected_beta(S_pil, S_ref_0, pay_frob2_sum, P_target):
    59	    """
    60	    Exact expected transmit power normalization:
    61	      E_{d_pay}[ || S_pil + beta * (S_ref_0 + sum_{k,m>=1} d_{k,m} M_{k,m}) ||_F^2 ] == P_target
    62	    Since d_{k,m} (m>=1) are i.i.d. zero-mean unit-variance 16-QAM symbols:
    63	      E[||S_tot||_F^2] = ||S_pil||_F^2 + 2*beta*Re<S_pil, S_ref_0> + beta^2 * (||S_ref_0||_F^2 + pay_frob2_sum).
    64	    """
    65	    e_pil = float(np.sum(np.abs(S_pil) ** 2))
    66	    a_coef = float(np.sum(np.abs(S_ref_0) ** 2)) + float(pay_frob2_sum)
    67	    b_re = float(np.real(np.vdot(S_pil, S_ref_0)))
    68	    c_coef = e_pil - float(P_target)
    69	    if a_coef <= 1e-18:
    70	        return 0.0
    71	    disc = max(b_re * b_re - a_coef * c_coef, 0.0)
    72	    return float(max((-b_re + np.sqrt(disc)) / a_coef, 0.0))

   195	# DDAM guard geometry: D_ddam=[19..58] under ALL TX/RX shifts 4*(el_tx - el_rx) in [-8..+8] lands in [11..63] U [0..2],
   196	# leaving [3..10] strictly disjoint for aligned pilot m_p_ddam=6, obs_ddam=[4..8]!
   197	m_p_ddam = 6
   198	obs_ddam = np.arange(4, 9)
   199	D_ddam = np.arange(19, 59)
   200	
   201	P_of_comb = np.arange(0, N, 8)
   202	G_of_comb = np.unique(np.concatenate([(P_of_comb - 1) % N, P_of_comb, (P_of_comb + 1) % N]))
   203	D_of_comb = np.setdiff1d(np.arange(N), G_of_comb)
   204	
   205	rng_hc = np.random.default_rng(20261125)
   206	best_p_hc = np.sort(rng_hc.choice(N, size=8, replace=False))
   207	def _sl_hc(idx):
   208	    v = np.zeros(N); v[idx] = 1.0
   209	    return float(np.max(np.abs(np.fft.ifft(v))[1:6]) * N / len(idx))
   210	best_v = _sl_hc(best_p_hc)
   211	for _ in range(800):
   212	    c_idx = best_p_hc.copy()
   213	    c_idx[rng_hc.integers(0, 8)] = rng_hc.choice(np.setdiff1d(np.arange(N), c_idx))
   214	    c_idx.sort()
   215	    sv = _sl_hc(c_idx)
   216	    if sv < best_v:
   217	        best_v, best_p_hc = sv, c_idx
   218	P_of_hc = best_p_hc
   219	g_pool = np.setdiff1d(np.unique(np.concatenate([(P_of_hc - 1) % N, (P_of_hc + 1) % N, (P_of_hc - 2) % N, (P_of_hc + 2) % N])), P_of_hc)
   220	if len(g_pool) < 16:
   221	    g_pool = np.concatenate([g_pool, np.setdiff1d(np.arange(N), np.concatenate([P_of_hc, g_pool]))[:16 - len(g_pool)]])
   222	G_of_hc = np.sort(np.concatenate([P_of_hc, g_pool[:16]]))
   223	D_of_hc = np.setdiff1d(np.arange(N), G_of_hc)
   224	assert len(D_af) == N_act and len(D_ddam) == N_act and len(D_of_comb) == N_act and len(D_of_hc) == N_act
   225	
   226	P_tot = float(N)
   227	E_pilot = (24.0 / 64.0) * P_tot
   228	P_comm = (40.0 / 64.0) * P_tot
   229	theta_tar = 40.0

   337	def build_radar_null_beam(Nt_v, K_v, est_s1, null_users=True):
   338	    a_tar = steering_ula(Nt_v, theta_tar)
   339	    if not null_users:
   340	        return a_tar / max(float(np.linalg.norm(a_tar)), 1e-12)
   341	    A_u = np.column_stack([steering_ula(Nt_v, th_j) for j in range(K_v) for (_, th_j, _, _) in est_s1[j]])
   342	    Q_u, R_u = np.linalg.qr(A_u, mode="reduced")
   343	    Q_u = Q_u[:, np.abs(np.diag(R_u)) > 1e-8]
   344	    wt = a_tar - Q_u @ (Q_u.conj().T @ a_tar)
   345	    if float(np.linalg.norm(wt)) < 1e-6:
   346	        wt = a_tar
   347	    return wt / max(float(np.linalg.norm(wt)), 1e-12)
   348	
   349	
   350	def align_add_radar_pilot_vec(v_u, wt, E_pil_target):
   351	    v_u_nrm2 = float(np.vdot(v_u, v_u).real)
   352	    if v_u_nrm2 >= E_pil_target:
   353	        return v_u * np.sqrt(E_pil_target / v_u_nrm2)
   354	    inner = np.vdot(wt, v_u)
   355	    wt_aligned = wt * np.exp(1j * np.angle(inner + 1e-15))
   356	    b_re = float(np.abs(inner))
   357	    c_val = v_u_nrm2 - E_pil_target
   358	    alpha_rad = max(-b_re + np.sqrt(max(b_re ** 2 - c_val, 0.0)), 0.0)
   359	    return alpha_rad * wt_aligned + v_u
   360	
   361	
   362	def design_beam_matched_dual_isac(Nt_v, K_v, e_u_pil, est_s1, sig2_h, null_inter_user=True):
   363	    dths = [-0.9, 0.0, 0.9]
   364	    Wk = np.zeros((K_v, Nt_v), dtype=complex)
   365	    for k in range(K_v):
   366	        if null_inter_user:
   367	            Ri = max(K_v * sig2_h / P_comm, 1e-4) * np.eye(Nt_v, dtype=complex)
   368	            for j in range(K_v):
   369	                if j == k:
   370	                    continue
   371	                for (_, th_j, _, al_j) in est_s1[j]:
   372	                    Asp = sum(np.outer(steering_ula(Nt_v, th_j + dt), steering_ula(Nt_v, th_j + dt).conj()) for dt in dths) / 3.0
   373	                    Ri += 120.0 * (abs(al_j) ** 2 + 0.03) * Asp
   374	            L = np.linalg.cholesky(np.linalg.inv(Ri))
   375	        else:
   376	            L = np.eye(Nt_v, dtype=complex)
   377	        Ak_c = np.array([steering_ula(Nt_v, th_k).conj() for (_, th_k, _, _) in est_s1[k]])
   378	        Beff = Ak_c @ L
   379	        ph_k = np.zeros(Pk)
   380	        for _ in range(30):
   381	            wc = L @ (Beff.conj().T @ np.exp(1j * ph_k))
   382	            wc /= max(float(np.linalg.norm(wc)), 1e-12)
   383	            ph_k = np.angle(Ak_c @ wc)
   384	        Wk[k] = wc
   385	
   386	    wt = build_radar_null_beam(Nt_v, K_v, est_s1, null_users=null_inter_user)
   387	    v_u = sum(np.sqrt(e_u_pil) * Wk[k] for k in range(K_v))
   388	    s_pil_vec = align_add_radar_pilot_vec(v_u, wt, E_pilot)
   389	    return s_pil_vec, Wk, wt
   390

   392	def solve_liu26_cpi_perbin_wmmse_extrap(Nt_v, K_v, est_s1, sig2_h, Dt):
   393	    """
   394	    Rang Liu & Schober (arXiv:2607.05119) Sec. IV-B CPI-fixed per-bin WMMSE beamformer
   395	    designed on Stage-1 extrapolated CSI across q = 0..Q-1 (20 WMMSE iterations).
   396	    NOTE (Explicit Pre-Registered Generosity to Baseline): Liu & Schober Eq. (33) assumes shared CSI
   397	    so UE applies U_rx_q[q][k] (2560 complex scalars/block of unpriced downlink control signaling granted
   398	    for free ONLY to this baseline; pilot subtraction still uses pure UE-blind obs_af estimation).
   399	    """
   400	    Hk_ext = [[np.zeros((N, N_act * Nt_v), dtype=complex) for _ in range(Q)] for _ in range(K_v)]
   401	    for k in range(K_v):
   402	        for p, (el, th_h, nu_h, al_m1) in enumerate(est_s1[k]):
   403	            Ec = make_E_op(A_af, c1_af, el, nu_h)[:, Dt]
   404	            H_b = np.kron(Ec, steering_ula(Nt_v, th_h).conj()[None, :])
   405	            for q in range(Q):
   406	                Hk_ext[k][q] += (al_m1 * np.exp(1j * 2 * np.pi * nu_h * (q + 1) * Tsym)) * H_b
   407	
   408	    W_bin = np.zeros((K_v, N_act, Nt_v), dtype=complex)
   409	    for k in range(K_v):
   410	        for m in range(N_act):
   411	            W_bin[k, m] = Hk_ext[k][0][Dt[m], m * Nt_v:(m + 1) * Nt_v].conj()
   412	    nrm0 = np.sqrt(max(float(np.sum(np.abs(W_bin) ** 2)), 1e-12))
   413	    W_bin *= np.sqrt(P_comm) / nrm0
   414	
   415	    def _exp_act(Wb_k):
   416	        M = np.zeros((N_act * Nt_v, N_act), dtype=complex)
   417	        for m in range(N_act):
   418	            M[m * Nt_v:(m + 1) * Nt_v, m] = Wb_k[m]
   419	        return M
   420	
   421	    U_rx_final = [[None] * K_v for _ in range(Q)]
   422	    for it in range(20):
   423	        Wc_list = [_exp_act(W_bin[k]) for k in range(K_v)]
   424	        Tw_d = np.zeros((N_act, Nt_v, Nt_v), dtype=complex)
   425	        Bk_d = np.zeros((K_v, N_act, Nt_v), dtype=complex)
   426	        for q in range(Q):
   427	            for k in range(K_v):
   428	                HW_k = [Hk_ext[k][q] @ Wc_list[j] for j in range(K_v)]
   429	                Ck = sig2_h * np.eye(N) + sum(HW_k[j] @ HW_k[j].conj().T for j in range(K_v))
   430	                Uk = np.linalg.solve(Ck, HW_k[k])
   431	                U_rx_final[q][k] = Uk
   432	                if it == 19:
   433	                    continue
   434	                Ek = np.eye(N_act) - HW_k[k].conj().T @ Uk
   435	                Ek = 0.5 * (Ek + Ek.conj().T) + 1e-7 * np.eye(N_act)
   436	                Mk = np.linalg.inv(Ek)
   437	                HUMU = Uk @ Mk @ Uk.conj().T
   438	                Tw_f = (1.0 / Q) * (Hk_ext[k][q].conj().T @ HUMU @ Hk_ext[k][q])
   439	                Bk_f = (1.0 / Q) * (Hk_ext[k][q].conj().T @ (Uk @ Mk))
   440	                for m in range(N_act):
   441	                    sl = slice(m * Nt_v, (m + 1) * Nt_v)
   442	                    Tw_d[m] += Tw_f[sl, sl]
   443	                    Bk_d[k, m] += Bk_f[sl, m]
   444	        if it == 19:
   445	            break
   446	        evs_a, Us_a, Bp_a = [], [], []
   447	        for m in range(N_act):
   448	            ev, Ue = np.linalg.eigh(0.5 * (Tw_d[m] + Tw_d[m].conj().T))
   449	            evs_a.append(np.maximum(ev, 0.0)); Us_a.append(Ue)
   450	            Bp_a.append(Ue.conj().T @ Bk_d[:, m, :].T)
   451	        ec = np.concatenate(evs_a); bc = np.vstack(Bp_a); rp = np.sum(np.abs(bc) ** 2, axis=1)
   452	        lo, hi = 1e-7, 1e4
   453	        for _ in range(36):
   454	            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
   455	            if float(np.sum(rp / ((ec + mid) ** 2))) > P_comm:
   456	                lo = mid
   457	            else:
   458	                hi = mid
   459	        for m in range(N_act):
   460	            Wm = Us_a[m] @ ((1.0 / (evs_a[m] + hi))[:, None] * Bp_a[m])
   461	            for k in range(K_v):
   462	                W_bin[k, m] = Wm[:, k]
   463	    return W_bin, U_rx_final
   464

   470	def lmmse_slice_payload(yd, Gk, sig2_b, ref_sym=1.0 + 0.0j):
   471	    GhG = Gk.conj().T @ Gk
   472	    Minv = np.linalg.inv(GhG + sig2_b * np.eye(N_act))
   473	    dku = (Minv @ (Gk.conj().T @ yd)) / np.maximum(np.real(np.diag(Minv @ GhG)), 1e-8)
   474	    _ = ref_sym  # Reference bin m=0 excluded from payload error count (39/64 net payload)
   475	    return pts16[np.argmin(np.abs(dku[1:, None] - pts16[None, :]), axis=1)]

   478	def blind_est_afdm_channel(yk, xp_af, af_bank):
   479	    """Pure UE-local blind 3-path (gain, Doppler, noise_var) estimator from yk[obs_af] alone."""
   480	    nus_h = []
   481	    for el in [0, 1, 2]:
   482	        cb = m_p_af - 4 * el
   483	        wl = np.arange(max(0, cb - 1), min(13, cb + 2))
   484	        bi = max(
   485	            range(len(NU_CAND_RX)),
   486	            key=lambda i: abs(np.vdot(af_bank[el][i][wl], yk[wl])) ** 2 / max(float(np.vdot(af_bank[el][i][wl], af_bank[el][i][wl]).real), 1e-12),
   487	        )
   488	        nb = float(NU_CAND_RX[bi])
   489	        best_sc = abs(np.vdot(af_bank[el][bi][wl], yk[wl])) ** 2 / max(float(np.vdot(af_bank[el][bi][wl], af_bank[el][bi][wl]).real), 1e-12)
   490	        for nf in np.linspace(nb - 0.04, nb + 0.04, 7):
   491	            v = (make_E_op(A_af, c1_af, el, float(nf)) @ xp_af)[wl]
   492	            sc = abs(np.vdot(v, yk[wl])) ** 2 / max(float(np.vdot(v, v).real), 1e-12)
   493	            if sc > best_sc:
   494	                best_sc, nb = sc, float(nf)
   495	        nus_h.append(nb)
   496	
   497	    Eops = [make_E_op(A_af, c1_af, el, nus_h[el]) for el in [0, 1, 2]]
   498	    Phi = np.column_stack([(Eops[el] @ xp_af)[obs_af] for el in [0, 1, 2]])
   499	    gh = np.linalg.solve(Phi.conj().T @ Phi + 1e-6 * np.eye(3), Phi.conj().T @ yk[obs_af])
   500	    sig2_b = max(float(np.mean(np.abs(yk[obs_af] - Phi @ gh) ** 2)), 1e-5)
   501	    y_pil_hat = sum(gh[el] * (Eops[el] @ xp_af) for el in [0, 1, 2])
   502	    return gh, nus_h, Eops, sig2_b, y_pil_hat
   503

   505	def blind_user_block_rx_afdm(yk, xp_af, af_bank, sc_data):
   506	    gh, _, Eops, sig2_b, y_pil_hat = blind_est_afdm_channel(yk, xp_af, af_bank)
   507	    yd = yk - y_pil_hat
   508	    Gk = sc_data * sum(gh[el] * Eops[el][:, D_af] for el in [0, 1, 2])
   509	    return lmmse_slice_payload(yd, Gk, sig2_b)

   512	def blind_est_ofdm_sol_jls(yk, xp_u_of, grid_jls, w_jls, G_tones):
   513	    """Pure UE-local Sol upgraded weighted 3-round QR joint nonlinear LS estimator from yk[G_tones] alone."""
   514	    yw = yk[G_tones] * w_jls
   515	    g_nrm2 = np.maximum(np.sum(np.abs(grid_jls) ** 2, axis=2), 1e-12)
   516	    ix = [int(np.argmax((np.abs(grid_jls[j].conj() @ yw) ** 2) / g_nrm2[j])) for j in range(Pk)]
   517	    for _ in range(3):
   518	        for j in range(Pk):
   519	            B = np.column_stack([grid_jls[i, ix[i]] for i in range(Pk) if i != j])
   520	            Qo, _ = np.linalg.qr(B, mode="reduced")
   521	            gp = grid_jls[j] - ((grid_jls[j] @ Qo.conj()) @ Qo.T)
   522	            denom = np.maximum(np.sum(np.abs(gp) ** 2, axis=1), 1e-12)
   523	            ix[j] = int(np.argmax((np.abs(gp.conj() @ yw) ** 2) / denom))
   524	    A_mat = np.column_stack([grid_jls[j, ix[j]] for j in range(Pk)])
   525	    co, _, _, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(A_mat, yw, rcond=None)
   526	    H_full = sum(co[j] * make_E_op(F_of, 0.0, j, float(FREQS_OF_JLS[ix[j]])) for j in range(Pk))
   527	    sig2_b = max(float(np.mean(np.abs(yk[G_tones] - (H_full @ xp_u_of)[G_tones]) ** 2)), 1e-5)
   528	    y_pil_hat = H_full @ xp_u_of
   529	    return H_full, sig2_b, y_pil_hat
   530	
   531	
   532	def blind_user_block_rx_ofdm_sol_jls(yk, xp_u_of, grid_jls, w_jls, G_tones, D_tones, sc_data):
   533	    H_full, sig2_b, y_pil_hat = blind_est_ofdm_sol_jls(yk, xp_u_of, grid_jls, w_jls, G_tones)
   534	    yd = yk - y_pil_hat
   535	    Gk = sc_data * H_full[:, D_tones]
   536	    return lmmse_slice_payload(yd, Gk, sig2_b)

   539	def blind_user_block_rx_tx_rzf(yk, is_ofdm, xp_af, af_bank, xp_u_of, grid_jls, w_jls, G_tones, D_tones, ref_sym):
   540	    """
   541	    Pure UE-local receiver for BS-side ExtrapBlockRZF (AFDM & OFDM):
   542	    ZERO BS-side variables passed! Blindly subtracts pilot spillover from yk[obs_af]/yk[G_tones],
   543	    estimates scalar gain from in-band reference symbol Dt[0]=ref_sym, and slices Dt[1:].
   544	    """
   545	    if not is_ofdm:
   546	        _, _, _, _, y_pil_hat = blind_est_afdm_channel(yk, xp_af, af_bank)
   547	    else:
   548	        _, _, y_pil_hat = blind_est_ofdm_sol_jls(yk, xp_u_of, grid_jls, w_jls, G_tones)
   549	    yd = yk - y_pil_hat
   550	    zk_raw = yd[D_tones]
   551	    g_ref = (zk_raw[0] / ref_sym) if abs(zk_raw[0]) > 1e-3 else 1.0
   552	    zk_c = zk_raw[1:] / g_ref
   553	    return pts16[np.argmin(np.abs(zk_c[:, None] - pts16[None, :]), axis=1)]
   554

   556	def blind_user_block_rx_ddam(yk, xp_dd, dd_bank, sc_dd_ratio, ref_sym):
   557	    """
   558	    Pure UE-local receiver for DDAM (Lu & Zeng TWC'24):
   559	    ZERO BS-side variables passed! Both pilot (at m_p_ddam=6) and data (at D_ddam=[19..58]) traverse
   560	    the identical BS delay-Doppler alignment operator T_k(q). UE blindly estimates the aligned composite
   561	    tap's residual Doppler & gain on clean window obs_ddam=[4..8], calibrates per-block gain scale via
   562	    unitary projection on reference D_ddam[0], subtracts pilot, and runs full-block LMMSE.
   563	    """
   564	    bi = max(
   565	        range(len(NU_CAND_RX)),
   566	        key=lambda i: abs(np.vdot(dd_bank[i][obs_ddam], yk[obs_ddam])) ** 2 / max(float(np.vdot(dd_bank[i][obs_ddam], dd_bank[i][obs_ddam]).real), 1e-12),
   567	    )
   568	    nb = float(NU_CAND_RX[bi])
   569	    best_sc = abs(np.vdot(dd_bank[bi][obs_ddam], yk[obs_ddam])) ** 2 / max(float(np.vdot(dd_bank[bi][obs_ddam], dd_bank[bi][obs_ddam]).real), 1e-12)
   570	    for nf in np.linspace(nb - 0.04, nb + 0.04, 7):
   571	        v = (make_E_op(A_af, c1_af, 0, float(nf)) @ xp_dd)[obs_ddam]
   572	        sc = abs(np.vdot(v, yk[obs_ddam])) ** 2 / max(float(np.vdot(v, v).real), 1e-12)
   573	        if sc > best_sc:
   574	            best_sc, nb = sc, float(nf)
   575	
   576	    E0 = make_E_op(A_af, c1_af, 0, nb)
   577	    v0 = (E0 @ xp_dd)[obs_ddam]
   578	    gh0 = np.vdot(v0, yk[obs_ddam]) / max(float(np.vdot(v0, v0).real), 1e-12)
   579	    sig2_b = max(float(np.mean(np.abs(yk[obs_ddam] - gh0 * v0) ** 2)), 1e-5)
   580	    yd = yk - gh0 * (E0 @ xp_dd)
   581	
   582	    gamma_nom = sc_dd_ratio * gh0
   583	    z_ref = np.vdot(E0[:, D_ddam[0]], yd) / ref_sym
   584	    var_prior = 0.08 * max(abs(gamma_nom) ** 2, 1e-4)
   585	    gamma_hat = (var_prior * z_ref + sig2_b * gamma_nom) / (var_prior + sig2_b)
   586	
   587	    Gk0 = gamma_hat * E0[:, D_ddam]
   588	    return lmmse_slice_payload(yd, Gk0, sig2_b, ref_sym)

   591	def unified_pure_blue_rd_score(Y_rad_cpi, S_tot_cpi, Nt_v, is_ofdm, is_dyn, win_center, sub_cpi_len):
   592	    """
   593	    Single unified monostatic radar scoring function across ALL arms and ALL sub_cpi_len (Q=16 and Q=2):
   594	      1. ZERO CLEAN peak deletion, ZERO row masking.
   595	      2. 100% identical clutter Doppler grid fn_clut and QR orthogonal subspace projection (1e-6 rank threshold)
   596	         for BOTH sub_cpi_len=16 and sub_cpi_len=2 (ZERO q_len branching!).
   597	      3. ZERO peak-driven background mask: identical full-map mean background denominator bg = mean(rd_map_acc)
   598	         for both win_score and blind_score.
   599	      4. Returns exact (tot_proj_e, tot_raw_e) summed over the full 16-block CPI so proj_keep_db is computed
   600	         strictly as total post-projection energy / total pre-projection energy.
   601	    """
   602	    ar_t = steering_ula(Nr_rad, theta_tar)
   603	    at_t = steering_ula(Nt_v, theta_tar)
   604	    Y_beam_all = (Y_rad_cpi @ ar_t.conj()) / np.sqrt(float(Nr_rad))
   605	    n_subs = Q // sub_cpi_len
   606	
   607	    rd_map_acc = np.zeros((len(F_RAD_GRID), N_RAD_RANGE))
   608	    jw, lw = win_center
   609	    tot_proj_e = 0.0
   610	    tot_raw_e = 0.0
   611	    fn_clut = FN_CLUT_DYN if is_dyn else FN_CLUT_STA
   612	    clut_et = CLUT_E_CACHE[(is_ofdm, is_dyn)]
   613	    rad_et = RAD_E_CACHE[(is_ofdm, is_dyn)]
   614	
   615	    for isub in range(n_subs):
   616	        sl = slice(isub * sub_cpi_len, (isub + 1) * sub_cpi_len)
   617	        Y_sub = Y_beam_all[sl]
   618	        S_sub = S_tot_cpi[sl]
   619	        q_len = sub_cpi_len
   620	
   621	        S_flat = S_sub.reshape(q_len * N, Nt_v)
   622	        _, sv_s, Vh_s = np.linalg.svd(S_flat, full_matrices=False)
   623	        n_sp = min(6, int(np.sum(sv_s > 0.01 * sv_s[0])))
   624	        U_sp_modes = (S_flat @ Vh_s[:n_sp].conj().T).reshape(q_len, N, n_sp)
   625	        q_idx = np.arange(q_len)[:, None]
   626	        clut_cols = []
   627	        for il in range(N_RAD_RANGE):
   628	            for ic, fnc in enumerate(fn_clut):
   629	                Ec_T = clut_et[il][ic]
   630	                ph_q = np.exp(1j * 2 * np.pi * float(fnc) * q_idx * Tsym)
   631	                for isp in range(n_sp):
   632	                    clut_cols.append((ph_q * (U_sp_modes[:, :, isp] @ Ec_T)).ravel())
   633	        B_clut = np.column_stack(clut_cols)
   634	        Qb, Rb = np.linalg.qr(B_clut, mode="reduced")
   635	        diag_r = np.abs(np.diag(Rb))
   636	        Qb = Qb[:, diag_r > 1e-6 * max(float(np.max(diag_r)), 1e-12)]
   637	
   638	        y_vec = Y_sub.ravel()
   639	        y_proj = y_vec - Qb @ (Qb.conj().T @ y_vec)
   640	        u_tx = S_sub @ at_t.conj()
   641	        ph_rad_all = np.exp(1j * 2 * np.pi * F_RAD_GRID[:, None] * (q_idx.T) * Tsym)  # (36, q_len)
   642	
   643	        for il in range(N_RAD_RANGE):
   644	            # Vectorized across all 36 Doppler bins for range bin il:
   645	            T_mat = np.empty((len(F_RAD_GRID), q_len * N), dtype=complex)
   646	            for jf in range(len(F_RAD_GRID)):
   647	                T_mat[jf] = (ph_rad_all[jf][:, None] * (u_tx @ rad_et[il][jf])).ravel()
   648	            raw_es = np.maximum(np.sum(np.abs(T_mat) ** 2, axis=1), 1e-18)
   649	            T_proj = T_mat - (T_mat @ Qb.conj()) @ Qb.T
   650	            proj_es = np.maximum(np.sum(np.abs(T_proj) ** 2, axis=1), 1e-18)
   651	            if il == lw:
   652	                tot_proj_e += float(proj_es[jw])
   653	                tot_raw_e += float(raw_es[jw])
   654	            corr = T_proj.conj() @ y_proj
   655	            rd_map_acc[:, il] += (np.abs(corr) ** 2) / proj_es
   656	
   657	    bg_common = max(float(np.mean(rd_map_acc)), 1e-20)
   658	    win_peak = float(np.max(rd_map_acc[max(0, jw - 1):min(len(F_RAD_GRID), jw + 2), lw]))
   659	    j_pk, l_pk = np.unravel_index(int(np.argmax(rd_map_acc)), rd_map_acc.shape)
   660	    blind_peak = float(rd_map_acc[j_pk, l_pk])
   661	    return win_peak / bg_common, blind_peak / bg_common, (int(j_pk), int(l_pk)), float(tot_proj_e), float(tot_raw_e)
   662	
   663

   256	def build_rx_caches(K_cur):
   257	    e_u_pil = 0.55 * E_pilot / float(K_cur)
   258	    sc_data = np.sqrt(P_comm / float(K_cur * N_act))
   259	    xp_af = np.zeros(N, dtype=complex); xp_af[m_p_af] = np.sqrt(e_u_pil)
   260	    af_bank = [[make_E_op(A_af, c1_af, el, float(nc)) @ xp_af for nc in NU_CAND_RX] for el in range(Pk)]
   261	
   262	    e_dd_pil = 0.35 * E_pilot / float(K_cur * Pk)
   263	    xp_dd = np.zeros(N, dtype=complex); xp_dd[m_p_ddam] = np.sqrt(e_dd_pil)
   264	    dd_bank = [make_E_op(A_af, c1_af, 0, float(nc)) @ xp_dd for nc in NU_CAND_RX]
   265	
   266	    def _of_cache(P_tones, G_tones, edge_weight=0.6):
   267	        Lr = len(P_tones)
   268	        zc = np.exp(-1j * np.pi * np.arange(Lr) * (np.arange(Lr) + 1) / float(Lr))
   269	        xp_u = np.zeros(N, dtype=complex); xp_u[P_tones] = np.sqrt(e_u_pil / float(Lr)) * zc
   270	        ex = np.exp(2j * np.pi * FREQS_OF_JLS[:, None] * np.arange(N) / float(N))
   271	        s_td = np.fft.ifft(xp_u, norm="ortho")
   272	        pilots = np.array([(ex * np.roll(s_td, ell)[None, :]) @ F_of[G_tones].T for ell in range(Pk)])
   273	        w = np.where(np.isin(G_tones, P_tones), 1.0, edge_weight)
   274	        return zc, xp_u, pilots * w[None, None, :], w
   275

   283	def stage1_real_uplink_sounding(users, Nt_v, K_v, is_dyn, sig2_ul, rng, sound_block_idx=-1):
   284	    """Real Stage-1 uplink sounding reception & blind estimation (ZERO oracle parameter or sigma2 leaks!)."""
   285	    step_ul = N // K_v
   286	    m_uls = [(10 + k * step_ul) % N for k in range(K_v)]
   287	    E_ul_k = float(N)
   288	    Y_ul = np.sqrt(sig2_ul / 2.0) * (rng.standard_normal((N, Nt_v)) + 1j * rng.standard_normal((N, Nt_v)))
   289	    for k in range(K_v):
   290	        x_ul = np.zeros(N, dtype=complex); x_ul[m_uls[k]] = np.sqrt(E_ul_k)
   291	        for (el, _, _, s_als, s_nus, s_ths) in users[k]:
   292	            for m in range(M_sub):
   293	                b_m1 = s_als[m] * np.exp(1j * 2 * np.pi * (s_nus[m] if is_dyn else 0.0) * sound_block_idx * Tsym)
   294	                y_ray = b_m1 * (make_E_op(A_af, c1_af, el, s_nus[m] if is_dyn else 0.0) @ x_ul)
   295	                Y_ul += y_ray[:, None] * steering_ula(Nt_v, s_ths[m])[None, :]
   296	
   297	    A_grid = np.array([steering_ula(Nt_v, tg) for tg in TH_GRID_S1])
   298	    nu_list = NU_GRID_S1 if is_dyn else np.array([0.0])
   299	    est_all = []
   300	    used_bins = np.zeros(N, dtype=bool)
   301	    for k in range(K_v):
   302	        x_ul = np.zeros(N, dtype=complex); x_ul[m_uls[k]] = np.sqrt(E_ul_k)
   303	        u_est = []
   304	        for el in [0, 1, 2]:
   305	            center = (m_uls[k] - 4 * el) % N
   306	            win = np.arange(center - 1, center + 2) % N if K_v == 4 else np.arange(center - 2, center + 3) % N
   307	            used_bins[np.arange(center - 2, center + 3) % N] = True
   308	            Y_w = Y_ul[win, :]
   309	            th0 = float(TH_GRID_S1[int(np.argmax(np.sum(np.abs(Y_w @ A_grid.conj().T) ** 2, axis=0)))])
   310	            f_ths = np.linspace(th0 - 0.25, th0 + 0.25, 21)
   311	            A_fine = np.array([steering_ula(Nt_v, tg) for tg in f_ths])
   312	            th_hat = float(f_ths[int(np.argmax(np.sum(np.abs(Y_w @ A_fine.conj().T) ** 2, axis=0)))])
   313	            y_beam = (Y_w @ steering_ula(Nt_v, th_hat).conj()) / float(Nt_v)
   314	            best_nu, best_sc, best_al = 0.0, -1.0, 0.0j
   315	            for nu_c in nu_list:
   316	                v = (make_E_op(A_af, c1_af, el, float(nu_c)) @ x_ul)[win]
   317	                nrm2 = max(float(np.vdot(v, v).real), 1e-12)
   318	                al_c = np.vdot(v, y_beam) / nrm2
   319	                sc = (abs(al_c) ** 2) * nrm2
   320	                if sc > best_sc:
   321	                    best_sc, best_nu, best_al = sc, float(nu_c), al_c
   322	            if is_dyn:
   323	                for nu_f in np.linspace(best_nu - 0.025, best_nu + 0.025, 9):
   324	                    v = (make_E_op(A_af, c1_af, el, float(nu_f)) @ x_ul)[win]
   325	                    nrm2 = max(float(np.vdot(v, v).real), 1e-12)
   326	                    al_c = np.vdot(v, y_beam) / nrm2
   327	                    sc = (abs(al_c) ** 2) * nrm2
   328	                    if sc > best_sc:
   329	                        best_sc, best_nu, best_al = sc, float(nu_f), al_c
   330	            u_est.append((el, th_hat, best_nu, best_al))
   331	        est_all.append(u_est)
   332	    empty_mask = ~used_bins
   333	    sig2_ul_hat = max(float(np.mean(np.abs(Y_ul[empty_mask]) ** 2)) if np.any(empty_mask) else 1e-3, 1e-5)
   334	    return est_all, sig2_ul_hat

from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
source = Path(\"ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py\").read_text()
node = next(n for n in ast.parse(source).body if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name == \"newcombe_paired_binary_ci95\")
actual_ns, paper_ns = {\"np\": np}, {\"np\": np}
exec(compile(ast.Module(body=[node], type_ignores=[]), \"<audit>\", \"exec\"), actual_ns)
exec(ast.get_source_segment(source, node).replace(\"num - np.sign(num) * min(0.5 * n, abs(num))\", \"(max(num - 0.5 * n, 0.0) if num > 0.0 else num)\"), paper_ns)
def find():
    for a in range(40):
        for c in range(1, 25):
            for d in range(40):
                b = 144 - a - c - d
                if a*d >= b*c or not 0.607 <= (b-c)/144 <= 0.637:
                    continue
                x = [1]*(a+b) + [0]*(c+d)
                y = [1]*a + [0]*b + [1]*c + [0]*d
                old = actual_ns[node.name](x,y)

                    return (a,b,c,d,old,ref)
print(find())

orig = np.linalg.qr
noticed = []
def observe(a, *args, **kwargs):
    Q, R = orig(a, *args, **kwargs)
    if a.shape in ((128, 180), (1024, 180)) and a.shape not in [x[0] for x in noticed]:
        dr = np.abs(np.diag(R))
        U = Q[:, dr > 1e-6 * max(np.max(dr),1e-12)]
        error = np.linalg.norm(a - U @ (U.conj().T @ a))/np.linalg.norm(a)

        noticed.append((a.shape,U.shape[1],int(np.sum(sv>1e-6*sv[0])),error))
    return Q,R

codex
**暂缓在 `deinlab` 启动第 12 轮确证。** 配对置信区间的实现可以把 P2 的雷达子项从不通过变成通过。单场景复核还发现，短处理窗的杂波投影漏掉了本应去除的方向。两处问题都继承自第 11 轮。

### 阻断级

1. **[Blocking] ①④ 配对二元置信区间实现偏离 Newcombe Method 10。** 原文只在配对相关系数为正时做连续性修正，脚本也把负相关向零收缩，可能抬高 95% 置信下限。用 144 对固定计数 `(同中, 仅本法中, 仅对照中, 同未中) = (50, 93, 1, 0)` 复算，脚本下限为 `0.551318`，原法为 `0.548820`，两者半宽均合格。`0.55` 门槛因此给出相反判定，差异直接进入 `PASS_PRIMARY`。[脚本第 1033–1046 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1033) · [P2 第 1236–1247 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1236) · [Newcombe 原文第 2639 页](https://www.site.uottawa.ca/~nat/Courses/csi5388/Newcombe.1998.pdf)

2. **[Blocking] ①③ 短窗的杂波投影漏掉了字典中的方向。** 脚本用无列主元的 QR 分解筛选杂波基。在开发种子 `160000` 的一个无目标场景中，短窗的 `128×180` 杂波矩阵按相对奇异值阈值 `10⁻⁶` 有 `126` 个方向，现有投影只保留 `124` 个，投影后仍残留原字典 Frobenius 范数的 `8.48%`。残差进入雷达评分，影响对手的校准门限和 P2 的定位命中差。[脚本第 621–655 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:621) · [P2 第 1188 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1188)

3. **[Blocking] ④⑤ 长短相干处理窗的比较同时更换了发射预编码和上行探测节奏。** `Prop(Q=16)` 用路径解耦波束和一次上行探测，`Short8xQ2` 用正则化迫零预编码，每两块重测。两组共用雷达评分函数，但送进去的发射波形和回波已经不同。这个比较可衡量两套系统的差异。要按预注册把 P2 的定位增益归于处理窗长度，还缺一组对同一发射序列和回波只切换 `Q=16` 与 `8×Q=2` 的对照。[脚本第 694–709、878–920、973–991 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:694) · [预注册第 75–76 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/PREDICTIONS_h_r12_h02_conf.md:75)

4. **[Blocking] ①④⑤ `Abl_Bonly_MRT` 同时关掉了通信波束和雷达波束对用户的零陷。** 同一个开关既把通信波束改为最大比传输，也把雷达波束改为直接指向目标。后者会改变叠加导频在用户端的干扰，进而影响 P2 的消融恢复率和误符率差。`Abl_Aonly` 只冻结第零块的终端估计，符合单机制消融。[脚本第 337–347、362–389、703–704、933–968 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:337) · [P2 第 1190–1193 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1190)

5. **[Blocking] ④⑤ 两组归因诊断缺少预注册所要求的精确干预。** `UnagedQ0` 只测第零块，`Aged` 平均全部 16 块。后 15 块没有在同一真实信道、载荷和噪声下只替换信道估计年龄的配对样本。两组 `TrueEffH` 接收机虽然拿到真实路径和导频回波，但给数据有效信道乘的是固定的 `sc_data_nom`，实际发射用的是逐场景的 `beta_std`。`Prop` 和梳状 OFDM 的盲接收对照确实共享场景、空间波束和均衡后端，这两处缺口分别影响纯老化归因和已知信道归因。[脚本第 256–259、800–801、878–882、909–951 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:800) · [预注册第 62–63、76–79 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/PREDICTIONS_h_r12_h02_conf.md:62)

6. **[Blocking] ①④ P1 用同一批正式样本选对手并计算选中后的置信区间。** 静态与动态分别按正式样本的平均误符率挑选 OFDM 对手，随后按对手已固定计算区间。两个工况可选出不同算法，第 11 轮确实分别选了外推迫零和梳状 OFDM。这样计算的 95% 区间未计入对手选择，且双重差分不能解释为固定同一对手时的工况交互。该下限直接进入 P1。[脚本第 1163–1183、1228–1235 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1163) · [第 11 轮汇总第 169、310 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/out_h_r11_h02/summary_h_r11_h02.json:169)

7. **[Blocking] ① 正式输出新增了本轮授权范围外的失败路径。** 完整差异显示，结果文件从覆盖写改为独占创建。若目标文件已存在，脚本会在算完后抛出 `FileExistsError`，这次判决不会写入。预注册记载了这项改动，但本轮规则只批准调整 Liu26 迭代和种子。[第 11 轮脚本第 1296–1299 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r11_h02.py:1296) · [第 12 轮脚本第 1308–1311 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1308) · [本轮规则第 39 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/prompts/round-12.md:39)

### 非阻断级

- **[Non-blocking]** 预注册称 16 个动态开发场景已确认失败由相位老化造成。这批场景直接支持的结论是把 Liu26 从 4 轮增至 20 轮后，动态平均误符率仍为 `0.7299`。这句归因表述不进入判决。[预注册第 31 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/PREDICTIONS_h_r12_h02_conf.md:31) · [开发结果第 104–139 行](/Users/guoyikun/.cache/idea-funnel/afdm-20261125-h-r12/dev_liu26_20iter_check.json:104)

种子和盲接收接口核对通过。按实际偏移枚举，`500000 / 600000 / 700000` 生成的 `2,272` 个场景及上行探测随机流种子与开发集、第 11 轮均无交集。机械平移 `+100000` 会撞上旧 `Z1` 的 `300000` 段、旧 `S2` 的 `440000` 段及其上行探测随机流。盲接收路径只使用终端观测和公开帧参数，Liu26 的 `U_rx_liu` 符合预注册的例外。[种子生成第 1049–1052、1136–1156 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1049) · [接收分支第 933–968 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:933)

40 个独立开发场景中，Liu26 的加权最小均方误差迭代目标单调，20 轮静态平均误符率为 `0.00462`，95% 上限为 `0.00848`。正式样本量、八组静态 `<0.010` 门槛、`39+1+24` 格预算及每块期望能量 `64.0` 均保留。此次只执行了静态阅读、固定计数复算和一个本地开发场景的投影检查，没有启动正式实验。[开发结果第 2–51 行](/Users/guoyikun/.cache/idea-funnel/afdm-20261125-h-r12/dev_liu26_20iter_check.json:2) · [正式门槛第 1195–1247 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1195)

**暂缓在 `deinlab` 启动第 12 轮确证。** 配对置信区间的实现可以把 P2 的雷达子项从不通过变成通过。单场景复核还发现，短处理窗的杂波投影漏掉了本应去除的方向。两处问题都继承自第 11 轮。

### 阻断级

1. **[Blocking] ①④ 配对二元置信区间实现偏离 Newcombe Method 10。** 原文只在配对相关系数为正时做连续性修正，脚本也把负相关向零收缩，可能抬高 95% 置信下限。用 144 对固定计数 `(同中, 仅本法中, 仅对照中, 同未中) = (50, 93, 1, 0)` 复算，脚本下限为 `0.551318`，原法为 `0.548820`，两者半宽均合格。`0.55` 门槛因此给出相反判定，差异直接进入 `PASS_PRIMARY`。[脚本第 1033–1046 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1033) · [P2 第 1236–1247 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1236) · [Newcombe 原文第 2639 页](https://www.site.uottawa.ca/~nat/Courses/csi5388/Newcombe.1998.pdf)

2. **[Blocking] ①③ 短窗的杂波投影漏掉了字典中的方向。** 脚本用无列主元的 QR 分解筛选杂波基。在开发种子 `160000` 的一个无目标场景中，短窗的 `128×180` 杂波矩阵按相对奇异值阈值 `10⁻⁶` 有 `126` 个方向，现有投影只保留 `124` 个，投影后仍残留原字典 Frobenius 范数的 `8.48%`。残差进入雷达评分，影响对手的校准门限和 P2 的定位命中差。[脚本第 621–655 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:621) · [P2 第 1188 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1188)

3. **[Blocking] ④⑤ 长短相干处理窗的比较同时更换了发射预编码和上行探测节奏。** `Prop(Q=16)` 用路径解耦波束和一次上行探测，`Short8xQ2` 用正则化迫零预编码，每两块重测。两组共用雷达评分函数，但送进去的发射波形和回波已经不同。这个比较可衡量两套系统的差异。要按预注册把 P2 的定位增益归于处理窗长度，还缺一组对同一发射序列和回波只切换 `Q=16` 与 `8×Q=2` 的对照。[脚本第 694–709、878–920、973–991 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:694) · [预注册第 75–76 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/PREDICTIONS_h_r12_h02_conf.md:75)

4. **[Blocking] ①④⑤ `Abl_Bonly_MRT` 同时关掉了通信波束和雷达波束对用户的零陷。** 同一个开关既把通信波束改为最大比传输，也把雷达波束改为直接指向目标。后者会改变叠加导频在用户端的干扰，进而影响 P2 的消融恢复率和误符率差。`Abl_Aonly` 只冻结第零块的终端估计，符合单机制消融。[脚本第 337–347、362–389、703–704、933–968 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:337) · [P2 第 1190–1193 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1190)

5. **[Blocking] ④⑤ 两组归因诊断缺少预注册所要求的精确干预。** `UnagedQ0` 只测第零块，`Aged` 平均全部 16 块。后 15 块没有在同一真实信道、载荷和噪声下只替换信道估计年龄的配对样本。两组 `TrueEffH` 接收机虽然拿到真实路径和导频回波，但给数据有效信道乘的是固定的 `sc_data_nom`，实际发射用的是逐场景的 `beta_std`。`Prop` 和梳状 OFDM 的盲接收对照确实共享场景、空间波束和均衡后端，这两处缺口分别影响纯老化归因和已知信道归因。[脚本第 256–259、800–801、878–882、909–951 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:800) · [预注册第 62–63、76–79 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/PREDICTIONS_h_r12_h02_conf.md:62)

6. **[Blocking] ①④ P1 用同一批正式样本选对手并计算选中后的置信区间。** 静态与动态分别按正式样本的平均误符率挑选 OFDM 对手，随后按对手已固定计算区间。两个工况可选出不同算法，第 11 轮确实分别选了外推迫零和梳状 OFDM。这样计算的 95% 区间未计入对手选择，且双重差分不能解释为固定同一对手时的工况交互。该下限直接进入 P1。[脚本第 1163–1183、1228–1235 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1163) · [第 11 轮汇总第 169、310 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/out_h_r11_h02/summary_h_r11_h02.json:169)

7. **[Blocking] ① 正式输出新增了本轮授权范围外的失败路径。** 完整差异显示，结果文件从覆盖写改为独占创建。若目标文件已存在，脚本会在算完后抛出 `FileExistsError`，这次判决不会写入。预注册记载了这项改动，但本轮规则只批准调整 Liu26 迭代和种子。[第 11 轮脚本第 1296–1299 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r11_h02.py:1296) · [第 12 轮脚本第 1308–1311 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1308) · [本轮规则第 39 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/prompts/round-12.md:39)

### 非阻断级

- **[Non-blocking]** 预注册称 16 个动态开发场景已确认失败由相位老化造成。这批场景直接支持的结论是把 Liu26 从 4 轮增至 20 轮后，动态平均误符率仍为 `0.7299`。这句归因表述不进入判决。[预注册第 31 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/PREDICTIONS_h_r12_h02_conf.md:31) · [开发结果第 104–139 行](/Users/guoyikun/.cache/idea-funnel/afdm-20261125-h-r12/dev_liu26_20iter_check.json:104)

种子和盲接收接口核对通过。按实际偏移枚举，`500000 / 600000 / 700000` 生成的 `2,272` 个场景及上行探测随机流种子与开发集、第 11 轮均无交集。机械平移 `+100000` 会撞上旧 `Z1` 的 `300000` 段、旧 `S2` 的 `440000` 段及其上行探测随机流。盲接收路径只使用终端观测和公开帧参数，Liu26 的 `U_rx_liu` 符合预注册的例外。[种子生成第 1049–1052、1136–1156 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1049) · [接收分支第 933–968 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:933)

40 个独立开发场景中，Liu26 的加权最小均方误差迭代目标单调，20 轮静态平均误符率为 `0.00462`，95% 上限为 `0.00848`。正式样本量、八组静态 `<0.010` 门槛、`39+1+24` 格预算及每块期望能量 `64.0` 均保留。此次只执行了静态阅读、固定计数复算和一个本地开发场景的投影检查，没有启动正式实验。[开发结果第 2–51 行](/Users/guoyikun/.cache/idea-funnel/afdm-20261125-h-r12/dev_liu26_20iter_check.json:2) · [正式门槛第 1195–1247 行](/Users/guoyikun/Downloads/share/claode_birain/afdm-funnel-h/00_Inbox/AFDM-channel-estimation/ideas/l2/h_l2_r12_h02_conf.py:1195)
